<li>hover-accordion slice layout</li>
<li>pristine gapless bento grid</li>
<li>infinite brand marquee strip</li>
<li>turning polaroid arc</li>
<li>vertical rhythm lines</li>
<li>off-grid editorial layout</li>
<li>product UI panel stack</li>
<li>split testimonial quote wall</li>
<li>layered image crop frames</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Motion-Implied Language</h3><a id="user-content-motion-implied-language" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Motion-Implied Language" href="#motion-implied-language"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Choose exactly 2:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>scrubbing text reveal energy</li>
<li>pinned narrative section energy</li>
<li>staggered float-up energy</li>
<li>parallax image drift energy</li>
<li>smooth accordion expansion energy</li>
<li>cinematic fade-through energy</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">These are not coding instructions.
They are visual-direction cues the design should imply.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">13. WEBSITE REFERENCE RULE</h2><a id="user-content-13-website-reference-rule" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 13. WEBSITE REFERENCE RULE" href="#13-website-reference-rule"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Every generated website section image must clearly communicate:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>layout</li>
<li>hierarchy</li>
<li>spacing</li>
<li>typography scale</li>
<li>CTA priority</li>
<li>component styling</li>
<li>image treatment</li>
<li>overall design system</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">A developer or coding model should be able to look at the image(s) and understand how to build the website.</p>
<p dir="auto">Do not produce vague abstract artwork when the request is for frontend.
Default to real section comps.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">14. HERO MINIMALISM RULES</h2><a id="user-content-14-hero-minimalism-rules" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 14. HERO MINIMALISM RULES" href="#14-hero-minimalism-rules"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The hero must feel cinematic, clear, and intentional.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Absolute Hero Rules</h3><a id="user-content-absolute-hero-rules" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Absolute Hero Rules" href="#absolute-hero-rules"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>the hero must feel like a strong opening scene</li>
<li>keep the hero composition very clean</li>
<li>do not overcrowd the first viewport</li>
<li>the main headline must feel short and powerful</li>
<li>the hero headline should ideally stay within 1–3 lines</li>
<li>do not allow long wrapped hero headlines</li>
<li>if the headline starts becoming too long, reduce words instead of forcing more lines</li>
<li>keep supporting text concise</li>
<li>prioritize negative space and contrast</li>
<li>avoid stuffing the hero with pills, fake stats, badges, tiny logos, and nonsense detail</li>
<li>avoid extra micro-labels, control tags, system markers, or decorative utility text that does not meaningfully help the hero</li>
<li>keep the first screen readable on a small laptop without feeling overfilled</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Hero Cleanliness Rule</h3><a id="user-content-hero-cleanliness-rule" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Hero Cleanliness Rule" href="#hero-cleanliness-rule"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The hero should feel calm, premium, and immediately readable.</p>
<p dir="auto">Do:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>use a strong single focal point</li>
<li>keep the hierarchy obvious</li>
<li>let the hero breathe</li>
<li>keep the visual system tight and controlled</li>
<li>make the first screen feel polished and deliberate</li>
<li>keep the amount of visible content restrained enough that the hero still feels elegant on a smaller desktop viewport</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Do not:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>clutter the hero</li>
<li>create multiple competing focal points</li>
<li>overfill the hero with cards or micro-details</li>
<li>make the hero noisy or busy</li>
<li>add unnecessary labels like “00 orchestration layer” or similar pseudo-system text if it does not add real value</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Headline Rule</h3><a id="user-content-headline-rule" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Headline Rule" href="#headline-rule"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Strong preference:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>1 line if possible</li>
<li>2 lines very good</li>
<li>3 lines maximum in normal cases</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Avoid:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>4+ line hero headlines</li>
<li>paragraph-like hero copy</li>
<li>weak headline-to-subheadline contrast</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">15. RESPONSIVE FIRST-VIEW RULE</h2><a id="user-content-15-responsive-first-view-rule" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 15. RESPONSIVE FIRST-VIEW RULE" href="#15-responsive-first-view-rule"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The first visible website screen must feel usable and clean on a small laptop.</p>
<p dir="auto">This means:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>do not overload the above-the-fold area</li>
<li>do not force too many content blocks into the hero viewport</li>
<li>do not rely on giant nested panels that consume space without improving clarity</li>
<li>make the first section feel intentionally composed, not overstuffed</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">The hero and immediate first-view area should:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>show the main message clearly</li>
<li>show the primary CTA clearly</li>
<li>show the key visual clearly</li>
<li>avoid trying to expose the entire product in one crowded first view</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">A smaller laptop should still see:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>a clear headline</li>
<li>readable supporting text</li>
<li>clean spacing</li>
<li>a visible CTA</li>
<li>a believable, balanced visual focal point</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">16. ANTI-NESTED-BOX RULE</h2><a id="user-content-16-anti-nested-box-rule" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 16. ANTI-NESTED-BOX RULE" href="#16-anti-nested-box-rule"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Do not default to box-in-box-in-box layouts.</p>
<p dir="auto">Avoid:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>giant rounded section containers wrapping everything</li>
<li>cards inside larger cards inside outer cards</li>
<li>dashboard-like compartment stacking for no reason</li>
<li>nested boxed UI that makes the layout feel trapped</li>
<li>sections that are just one big bordered panel containing more bordered panels containing more bordered panels</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Use boxes only when they have a clear purpose.</p>
<p dir="auto">Prefer:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>open layouts</li>
<li>clearer whitespace</li>
<li>fewer but stronger containers</li>
<li>flatter hierarchy where appropriate</li>
<li>direct alignment and spacing instead of excessive enclosure</li>
<li>one primary framing move rather than many layered frames</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">A section should not feel like a prison of containers.
It should feel designed, open, and intentional.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">17. REDUCE MICRO-UI CLUTTER RULE</h2><a id="user-content-17-reduce-micro-ui-clutter-rule" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 17. REDUCE MICRO-UI CLUTTER RULE" href="#17-reduce-micro-ui-clutter-rule"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Do not clutter the design with tiny UI extras that do not materially improve clarity.</p>
<p dir="auto">Avoid:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>unnecessary pills</li>
<li>pseudo-system markers</li>
<li>fake control labels</li>
<li>decorative code-like tags</li>
<li>meaningless small metadata rows</li>
<li>filler chips</li>
<li>tiny badges everywhere</li>
<li>fake dashboard jargon</li>
<li>overdesigned labels that distract from the main layout</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Examples of things to avoid unless they are truly necessary:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>“00 orchestration layer”</li>
<li>tiny technical status pills</li>
<li>decorative runtime markers</li>
<li>overly specific pseudo-enterprise microcopy</li>
<li>filler operator/control-room labels that exist only to look complex</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Prefer:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>cleaner headings</li>
<li>fewer labels</li>
<li>real hierarchy</li>
<li>clearer spacing</li>
<li>simpler supporting text</li>
<li>stronger typography instead of decorative clutter</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">18. SECTION IMAGE GENERATION RULE</h2><a id="user-content-18-section-image-generation-rule" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 18. SECTION IMAGE GENERATION RULE" href="#18-section-image-generation-rule"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Inside Codex, treat each section as its own analyzable unit.</p>
<p dir="auto">If the user asks for:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>a hero only → generate 1 hero image</li>
<li>4 sections → generate 4 section images</li>
<li>8 sections → generate 8 section images</li>
<li>12 sections → generate 12 section images when reasonable</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">General preference:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>one section = one primary image</li>
<li>one complex section = one primary image + one or more optional detail images</li>
<li>one unclear section = regenerate it again as a fresh clean standalone image</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">This section-first generation rule exists to prevent:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>tiny unreadable text</li>
<li>tiny buttons</li>
<li>unclear spacing</li>
<li>weak extraction quality</li>
<li>lossy design-to-code translation</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">19. WEBSITE IMAGE SYSTEM RULE</h2><a id="user-content-19-website-image-system-rule" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 19. WEBSITE IMAGE SYSTEM RULE" href="#19-website-image-system-rule"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">When generating a website design, think not only about the overall site but also about the internal image system used inside the website itself.</p>
<p dir="auto">This may include:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>hero media</li>
<li>section images</li>
<li>editorial crops</li>
<li>product visuals</li>
<li>framed photography</li>
<li>layered image cards</li>
<li>gallery-like blocks</li>
<li>supporting visual panels</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">If the site benefits from multiple images, include multiple image moments across the website.</p>
<p dir="auto">Rules:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>image usage must feel deliberate</li>
<li>image count should match the complexity of the site</li>
<li>do not rely on one single hero image if many sections need visual support</li>
<li>keep image usage balanced and clean</li>
<li>all image moments must still feel like one coherent design world</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">20. FIXED MEDIA FRAME RULE</h2><a id="user-content-20-fixed-media-frame-rule" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 20. FIXED MEDIA FRAME RULE" href="#20-fixed-media-frame-rule"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Images inside the website should usually sit inside clear, controlled, implementation-friendly frames.</p>
<p dir="auto">Prefer:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>fixed-aspect media blocks</li>
<li>clearly framed image areas</li>
<li>repeatable media modules</li>
<li>consistent corner radius logic</li>
<li>stable visual proportions across similar sections</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Examples:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>hero image in a clearly bounded large frame</li>
<li>editorial crops using repeatable portrait or landscape ratios</li>
<li>card images with consistent proportions</li>
<li>gallery blocks with controlled aspect ratios</li>
<li>product images placed in stable intentional containers</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Avoid:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>random image sizes with no system</li>
<li>inconsistent proportions across similar modules</li>
<li>messy scaling</li>
<li>uncontrolled collage chaos unless explicitly requested</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">The goal is:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>visually strong images</li>
<li>inside a system a frontend model can realistically rebuild</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">21. TEXT EXTRACTION RULE</h2><a id="user-content-21-text-extraction-rule" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 21. TEXT EXTRACTION RULE" href="#21-text-extraction-rule"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">When text is readable in the generated section image, extract it and use it.</p>
<p dir="auto">Especially inspect and extract:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>hero headline</li>
<li>hero subheadline</li>
<li>CTA labels</li>
<li>section headings</li>
<li>pricing labels</li>
<li>feature names</li>
<li>testimonial names and roles if clearly shown</li>
<li>navbar labels</li>
<li>footer labels if relevant</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">If the text is too small to extract reliably:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>generate a closer extraction image</li>
<li>or generate a second clearer version of that section</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Do not ignore text extraction.
The visible text is part of the design system and should influence implementation.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">22. TYPOGRAPHY EXTRACTION RULE</h2><a id="user-content-22-typography-extraction-rule" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 22. TYPOGRAPHY EXTRACTION RULE" href="#22-typography-extraction-rule"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Do not only notice that typography “looks nice”.
Analyze it properly.</p>
<p dir="auto">Extract and observe:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>size relationships</li>
<li>weight relationships</li>
<li>line count</li>
<li>line height feel</li>
<li>tracking feel</li>
<li>serif vs sans behavior</li>
<li>display vs body contrast</li>
<li>section heading rhythm</li>
<li>CTA text scale</li>
<li>whether the design uses calm or aggressive type</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Use these findings during implementation.
Do not flatten typography into a generic coded hierarchy.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">23. SPACING EXTRACTION RULE</h2><a id="user-content-23-spacing-extraction-rule" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 23. SPACING EXTRACTION RULE" href="#23-spacing-extraction-rule"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Analyze spacing deliberately.</p>
<p dir="auto">Inspect:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>distance between headline and subheadline</li>
<li>distance between text and buttons</li>
<li>distance between cards</li>
<li>section top and bottom spacing</li>
<li>side gutters</li>
<li>card padding</li>
<li>image-to-text distance</li>
<li>navbar spacing</li>
<li>CTA block spacing</li>
<li>overall cadence across sections</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">The goal is not exact pixel OCR.
The goal is faithful spacing logic.</p>
<p dir="auto">Do not collapse the implementation into generic tight spacing if the generated design is more generous.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">24. BUTTON / COMPONENT EXTRACTION RULE</h2><a id="user-content-24-button--component-extraction-rule" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 24. BUTTON / COMPONENT EXTRACTION RULE" href="#24-button--component-extraction-rule"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Buttons and components must be analyzed, not guessed.</p>
<p dir="auto">Inspect:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>button size</li>
<li>button shape</li>
<li>button radius</li>
<li>fill vs outline behavior</li>
<li>icon usage</li>
<li>hover-implied mood</li>
<li>primary vs secondary hierarchy</li>
<li>card structure</li>
<li>badge usage</li>
<li>dividers</li>
<li>shadows</li>
<li>borders</li>
<li>pill logic</li>
<li>input styling if present</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">If button or card detail is too small, generate a closer image.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">25. COLOR EXTRACTION RULE</h2><a id="user-content-25-color-extraction-rule" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 25. COLOR EXTRACTION RULE" href="#25-color-extraction-rule"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Actively analyze and extract colors from the generated image(s).</p>
<p dir="auto">Inspect:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>background color</li>
<li>panel colors</li>
<li>accent colors</li>
<li>button fills</li>
<li>text color hierarchy</li>
<li>border color logic</li>
<li>shadow color mood</li>
<li>image tint / grade</li>
<li>gradient restraint or intensity</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">The implemented website should preserve the original color logic as closely as reasonably possible.</p>
<p dir="auto">Do not replace a carefully designed palette with generic default web colors.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">26. DESIGN-TO-CODE COPY DISCIPLINE</h2><a id="user-content-26-design-to-code-copy-discipline" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 26. DESIGN-TO-CODE COPY DISCIPLINE" href="#26-design-to-code-copy-discipline"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">After generating and analyzing the reference image(s), implement the website in a copy-oriented way.</p>
<p dir="auto">This means:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>follow the references closely</li>
<li>preserve layout logic</li>
<li>preserve spacing rhythm</li>
<li>preserve section ordering</li>
<li>preserve text/image balance</li>
<li>preserve typography mood</li>
<li>preserve component style</li>
<li>preserve overall visual cleanliness</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Do not drift into a different design direction during implementation.
Do not “improve” the design by replacing it with a generic coded layout.</p>
<p dir="auto">The goal is not:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>inspired by the image</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">The goal is:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>visually faithful to the image, translated into real frontend</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">27. ANTI-DRIFT IMPLEMENTATION RULE</h2><a id="user-content-27-anti-drift-implementation-rule" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 27. ANTI-DRIFT IMPLEMENTATION RULE" href="#27-anti-drift-implementation-rule"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">A common failure mode is design drift:
the generated images look strong, but the coded result becomes generic.</p>
<p dir="auto">Strictly avoid that.</p>
<p dir="auto">During implementation:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>do not simplify into default templates</li>
<li>do not replace distinctive sections with generic rows</li>
<li>do not compress generous spacing into dense layout</li>
<li>do not replace strong typography with plain hierarchy</li>
<li>do not remove the page’s visual identity for convenience</li>
<li>do not merge section logic into repetitive patterns that were not present in the source images</li>
<li>do not reintroduce nested-box complexity that was intentionally removed during analysis</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">The final coded result should still feel like the same website as the generated references.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">28. MISSING DETAIL RESOLUTION</h2><a id="user-content-28-missing-detail-resolution" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 28. MISSING DETAIL RESOLUTION" href="#28-missing-detail-resolution"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">When implementing from images, some details may still be unclear.</p>
<p dir="auto">Resolve ambiguity by following this order:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>preserve the visible design language</li>
<li>preserve layout and spacing logic</li>
<li>preserve component family</li>
<li>preserve mood and polish level</li>
<li>generate an extra detail image if needed</li>
<li>regenerate the section as a fresh standalone image if needed</li>
<li>only then choose the most implementation-friendly faithful version</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto">Do not fill ambiguity with generic defaults too quickly.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">29. ANTI-AI-SLOP RULES</h2><a id="user-content-29-anti-ai-slop-rules" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 29. ANTI-AI-SLOP RULES" href="#29-anti-ai-slop-rules"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Strictly avoid these patterns unless explicitly requested.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Layout slop</h3><a id="user-content-layout-slop" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Layout slop" href="#layout-slop"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>one giant unreadable collage</li>
<li>endless centered sections</li>
<li>identical card rows repeated section after section</li>
<li>cloned left-text/right-image blocks</li>
<li>fake complexity without hierarchy</li>
<li>decorative empty space with no purpose</li>
<li>cards-inside-cards-inside-cards</li>
<li>giant rounded wrapper sections around everything</li>
<li>overcompartmentalized dashboard framing</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Visual slop</h3><a id="user-content-visual-slop" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Visual slop" href="#visual-slop"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>default purple/blue AI gradients</li>
<li>too many glowing edges</li>
<li>floating blobs everywhere</li>
<li>glassmorphism stacked without reason</li>
<li>random futuristic details with no structure</li>
<li>over-rendered noise that hides the layout</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Typography slop</h3><a id="user-content-typography-slop" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Typography slop" href="#typography-slop"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>giant heading + weak tiny subcopy</li>
<li>too many font moods</li>
<li>awkward line breaks</li>
<li>lazy all-caps everywhere</li>
<li>generic gradient headline tricks</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Content slop</h3><a id="user-content-content-slop" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Content slop" href="#content-slop"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Avoid generic filler vibes like:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>unleash</li>
<li>elevate</li>
<li>revolutionize</li>
<li>next-gen</li>
<li>seamless</li>
<li>transformative platform</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Avoid fake brand slop:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Acme</li>
<li>Nexus</li>
<li>Flowbit</li>
<li>Quantumly</li>
<li>NovaCore</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Avoid fake complexity slop:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>pseudo-enterprise control labels</li>
<li>decorative system markers</li>
<li>filler status microcopy</li>
<li>fake operator / runtime / orchestration jargon unless truly central to the brand</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Density slop</h3><a id="user-content-density-slop" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Density slop" href="#density-slop"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>over-packed sections</li>
<li>card overload</li>
<li>tiny spacing between major sections</li>
<li>visually exhausting walls of content</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">30. TYPOGRAPHY-FIRST DISCIPLINE</h2><a id="user-content-30-typography-first-discipline" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 30. TYPOGRAPHY-FIRST DISCIPLINE" href="#30-typography-first-discipline"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Typography is a primary design material.</p>
<p dir="auto">Always ensure:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>clear size contrast</li>
<li>obvious reading order</li>
<li>strong display moments</li>
<li>readable body text</li>
<li>concise copy</li>
<li>section headings that reinforce structure</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">For editorial directions:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>let typography shape composition</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">For tech/product directions:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>let typography communicate trust and precision</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">31. SECTION RHYTHM RULE</h2><a id="user-content-31-section-rhythm-rule" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 31. SECTION RHYTHM RULE" href="#31-section-rhythm-rule"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">A high-end site does not feel like the same block repeated forever.</p>
<p dir="auto">Vary section rhythm across the page by changing:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>density</li>
<li>image-to-text ratio</li>
<li>alignment</li>
<li>scale</li>
<li>whitespace</li>
<li>card grouping</li>
<li>background intensity</li>
<li>visual tempo</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">But:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>keep the page coherent</li>
<li>keep spacing controlled</li>
<li>avoid random jumps</li>
<li>keep each section clean enough to analyze well</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">32. DENSITY &amp; SPACING DISCIPLINE</h2><a id="user-content-32-density--spacing-discipline" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 32. DENSITY &amp; SPACING DISCIPLINE" href="#32-density--spacing-discipline"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Do not make the website too dense.</p>
<p dir="auto">The page should breathe.</p>
<p dir="auto">Rules:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>use even section spacing</li>
<li>keep major section gaps controlled and intentional</li>
<li>allow negative space to create calmness</li>
<li>avoid one section feeling cramped while the next feels empty</li>
<li>smaller sections should still have enough surrounding space</li>
<li>prefer analyzable generous spacing over compressed compositions</li>
<li>do not fill every available area with extra UI</li>
<li>let simplicity do part of the design work</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">A premium website should feel:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>open</li>
<li>composed</li>
<li>balanced</li>
<li>confident</li>
<li>breathable</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Not:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>cramped</li>
<li>noisy</li>
<li>uneven</li>
<li>overfilled</li>
<li>visually exhausting</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">33. DEFAULT SECTION PACKS</h2><a id="user-content-33-default-section-packs" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 33. DEFAULT SECTION PACKS" href="#33-default-section-packs"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">4-section pack</h3><a id="user-content-4-section-pack" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 4-section pack" href="#4-section-pack"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>Hero</li>
<li>Features</li>
<li>Social proof / testimonial</li>
<li>CTA</li>
</ol>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">8-section pack</h3><a id="user-content-8-section-pack" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 8-section pack" href="#8-section-pack"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>Hero</li>
<li>Trust bar</li>
<li>Features</li>
<li>Product showcase</li>
<li>Benefits / use cases</li>
<li>Testimonials</li>
<li>Pricing</li>
<li>CTA</li>
</ol>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">12-section pack</h3><a id="user-content-12-section-pack" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 12-section pack" href="#12-section-pack"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>Hero</li>
<li>Trust bar</li>
<li>Feature grid</li>
<li>Product preview</li>
<li>Problem / solution</li>
<li>Benefits</li>
<li>Workflow</li>
<li>Metrics / proof / integration</li>
<li>Testimonials</li>
<li>Pricing</li>
<li>FAQ</li>
<li>CTA + footer</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto">In Codex, these should usually become section-by-section images, not one compressed sheet.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">34. MULTI-IMAGE CONSISTENCY RULE</h2><a id="user-content-34-multi-image-consistency-rule" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 34. MULTI-IMAGE CONSISTENCY RULE" href="#34-multi-image-consistency-rule"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">For multi-image websites, enforce:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>same brand world</li>
<li>same type scale logic</li>
<li>same spacing discipline</li>
<li>same CTA styling</li>
<li>same icon mood</li>
<li>same image treatment</li>
<li>same tonal language</li>
<li>same component family</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Image 2, 3, or 8 must not drift into a different website.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">35. CLARITY CHECK</h2><a id="user-content-35-clarity-check" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 35. CLARITY CHECK" href="#35-clarity-check"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Before finalizing, verify internally:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>Has the design been generated first?</li>
<li>Have all generated images been deeply analyzed?</li>
<li>Is the text readable enough?</li>
<li>If not, were extra detail images created?</li>
<li>Were enough images generated, or was the image count too lazy?</li>
<li>Were unclear sections regenerated as fresh standalone images instead of being cropped?</li>
<li>Is the hierarchy obvious?</li>
<li>Is the hero clean enough?</li>
<li>Is typography analyzed properly?</li>
<li>Are spacing relationships understood properly?</li>
<li>Are buttons and components extracted properly?</li>
<li>Are colors analyzed properly?</li>
<li>Is the design visually distinctive?</li>
<li>Is it free of obvious AI tells?</li>
<li>Can someone code from this faithfully?</li>
<li>If multiple images exist, do they clearly belong together?</li>
<li>Has Codex avoided compressing too many sections into one tiny image?</li>
<li>Was the analysis clean, structured, and specific?</li>
<li>Has unnecessary nested boxing been removed?</li>
<li>Is the first screen still clean and readable on a small laptop?</li>
<li>Have useless pills, labels, and fake technical micro-elements been reduced?</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto">If not, refine internally before output.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">36. RESPONSE BEHAVIOR</h2><a id="user-content-36-response-behavior" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 36. RESPONSE BEHAVIOR" href="#36-response-behavior"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">When the user asks for a website design in an image-to-code workflow:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>infer site type</li>
<li>infer number of sections</li>
<li>if image generation is available and visual quality is central, generate the design image(s) first</li>
<li>inside Codex, prefer one large image per section</li>
<li>generate additional detail/extraction images if text or components are too small</li>
<li>generate more images whenever that improves readability or extraction quality</li>
<li>do not be lazy with image count</li>
<li>do not crop old images for section extraction</li>
<li>regenerate sections as fresh standalone images when needed</li>
<li>choose a strong visual combination</li>
<li>choose 4 signature components</li>
<li>choose 2 motion-implied cues</li>
<li>enforce hero cleanliness and short hero line count</li>
<li>reduce unnecessary pills, labels, and micro-UI clutter</li>
<li>avoid cards-inside-cards-inside-cards and giant boxed section wrappers</li>
<li>keep the first screen readable and balanced on a small laptop</li>
<li>enforce strong image usage where appropriate</li>
<li>keep spacing generous, even, and analyzable</li>
<li>deeply and cleanly analyze all generated images</li>
<li>extract text, typography, spacing, buttons, colors, components, and layout logic</li>
<li>implement the website to match the generated references as closely as reasonably possible</li>
<li>create the final files only after the full analysis pass</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto">Do not ask unnecessary follow-up questions if a strong interpretation is possible.
Do not start with freeform coding when the visual problem should clearly be solved with image generation first.
Do not compress many sections into one unreadable image in Codex.
Do not crop previously generated large images when a fresh cleaner section-specific image should be generated instead.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">37. EXAMPLE INTERPRETATIONS</h2><a id="user-content-37-example-interpretations" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 37. EXAMPLE INTERPRETATIONS" href="#37-example-interpretations"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Example 1</h3><a id="user-content-example-1" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Example 1" href="#example-1"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">User:
“make me one hero section for an AI startup”</p>
<p dir="auto">Interpretation:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>generate 1 hero image</li>
<li>if needed, generate 1 closer extraction image for text/buttons</li>
<li>do not crop a small region out of a larger board</li>
<li>if more clarity is needed, regenerate the hero as a fresh cleaner standalone image</li>
<li>keep the hero calm and readable</li>
<li>avoid fake utility labels and nested cards</li>
<li>analyze headline, subheadline, CTA, spacing, colors, hero media</li>
<li>then implement the hero</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Example 2</h3><a id="user-content-example-2" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Example 2" href="#example-2"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">User:
“design me an 8-section landing page”</p>
<p dir="auto">Interpretation:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>generate 8 separate section images in Codex</li>
<li>one per section</li>
<li>generate extra detail images where necessary</li>
<li>deeply analyze all 8 sections</li>
<li>extract text, typography, spacing, buttons, colors, cards, structure</li>
<li>if one section is still unclear, regenerate that section again cleanly instead of cropping</li>
<li>keep sections open and not overboxed</li>
<li>then implement the full site from those references</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Example 3</h3><a id="user-content-example-3" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Example 3" href="#example-3"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">User:
“make a premium creative agency website with 4 sections”</p>
<p dir="auto">Interpretation:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>generate 4 separate section images in Codex</li>
<li>keep the hero very clean</li>
<li>ensure text remains readable</li>
<li>deeply analyze each section</li>
<li>do not use rough cutouts from the first renders</li>
<li>regenerate clearer section images if needed</li>
<li>avoid over-pilled microcopy and container overload</li>
<li>then implement the site from those 4 references</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">38. FINAL GOAL</h2><a id="user-content-38-final-goal" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 38. FINAL GOAL" href="#38-final-goal"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Generate website reference images that feel:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>premium</li>
<li>art-directed</li>
<li>clear</li>
<li>structured</li>
<li>readable</li>
<li>analyzable</li>
<li>memorable</li>
<li>anti-generic</li>
<li>implementation-friendly</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">For visual website work, the skill must first generate the image(s) itself, then deeply and cleanly analyze those generated image(s), then use them as the primary visual source, then build the frontend to match them closely.</p>
<p dir="auto">Inside Codex, if the user wants multiple sections, prefer separate large section images instead of one compressed multi-section board, so text, spacing, typography, buttons, and colors can be extracted properly.</p>
<p dir="auto">If a section still needs more clarity, generate an additional extraction-oriented image for that section.</p>
<p dir="auto">If more images would improve quality, generate more images.
Do not be lazy with image count.</p>
<p dir="auto">Do not crop previously generated images when a fresh section-specific image would preserve spacing, layout, and readability better.
Generate a new clean image instead.</p>
<p dir="auto">Avoid cards-inside-cards-inside-cards.
Avoid giant boxed wrappers around every section.
Avoid fake technical pills and decorative micro-labels.
Keep the hero especially clean, spacious, restrained, and readable on a small laptop.</p>
<p dir="auto">The result should be:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>strong as section images</li>
<li>strong as a design system</li>
<li>strong under deep analysis</li>
<li>and strong as implemented frontend</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">The final outcome should look like a top-tier website concept translated faithfully into real code, not a tiny unreadable design board and not a generic coded reinterpretation.</p>

      .user-mention[href$="/saketh-valueskins"]:before,
      .user-mention[href$="/saketh-valueskins"]:after {
